Never change his face, beard, hat, outfit, body size or age. Only one Big Earl in the video.

PLACE: a small corner bodega in the Bronx, New York, at night: shelves of snacks, a deli grill behind the counter, a bodega cat sleeping on the chips, neon lottery signs.

CAMERA: Raw real iPhone video filmed by Earl's friend walking right behind him (the friend is never seen). Starts walking, then handheld and casual. Natural bounce and shake, real autofocus, natural light, true-to-life colors. Looks like a real video posted on social media, not a movie.

0-3s — HOOK
The friend follows Big Earl from behind as he walks up to the place; Earl turns his head to the phone without stopping.
DIALOGUE 0-3s — BIG EARL: "New York bodega. They say the chopped cheese is the real New York sandwich."

3-10s — THE ORDER
The phone turns to a friendly bodega worker in a hoodie chopping meat on the grill.

DIALOGUE 6-8s — BIG EARL: "Two. And something for the cat."
DIALOGUE 8-10s — BODEGA GUY: "Ha! The cat eats better than me!"
Earl slowly tips his hat.

10-22s — THE TASTE
A hero roll stuffed with chopped beef, onions, melted cheese, lettuce and tomato wrapped in paper; Earl takes a big bite while the bodega cat watches him. Two people nearby whisper and point at him. The friend steps closer and the autofocus hunts for a second.
DIALOGUE 17-19s — FRIEND (off camera, laughing): "The cat is judging you, Earl!"

22-30s — PUNCHLINE
Earl pats his belly and looks into the phone. Completely deadpan.
DIALOGUE 23-26s — BIG EARL: "The cat approves."
DIALOGUE 27-29s — BIG EARL: "One more for the road."
The video ends as Earl winks once at the camera.

SOUND: grill scraping, fridge humming, door bell, city traffic outside. Big Earl's voice: deep, slow, calm American male voice with a light Texas drawl. All lines spoken clearly with lip-sync, in this order. Dialogue louder than background sound. No music.
